## ✉️ TICKET #14418 [tier A] — 2026-04-29T22:15:08+00:00 — Re: SuperCat / Magic Lite: 4/29 Follow-Up

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* magiclite.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* l1 - handled by frontline support, product - admin console, s4 - low, type: training

```
Hi Team,Thank you both for making the time today for the admin training walkthrough. It was great to formally connect, and I'm excited to be your main point of contact as we move into the live stage. We covered a lot of ground: the Admin Console overview, user management and territory codes, pricing and user group settings, order configuration, and eCat Online. Please don't hesitate to reach out with any questions as you explore.It might also be worthwhile to forward this email (or the recording) to Brittany so she has context on the inventory discussion and overall setup.Here is the recording for your reference: https://fathom.video/share/4uCVDjTnFPQyCaL2E6g_kSzF9tgjtE5-YOUR QUICK REFERENCE LINKSAdmin Console: https://supercat.supercatsolutions.com/malieCat Online [eOL]: https://supercat.supercatsolutions.com/mali/e/1/logineCat SALES REP (iPad App): https://apps.apple.com/us/app/ecat-sales-rep/id428032907SuperCat Admin Set-up Process: https://kylor-johnson.github.io/admin-overlay/#setup-processKnowledge Base: https://supercatsolutions.com/knowledgebaseACTION ITEMS
  
#OwnerAction ItemETA1Magic LiteConfirm 3–5 US reps for the initial beta invitesEOW2Magic LiteSend co-branded logo (ML + NSL) for the eCat catalog tileEOW4Magic LiteSpend time in Admin Console (Tools &gt; Company Settings), list out any questionsBefore May 135Magic LiteFollow up with Brittany on the inventory file update format/cadenceBefore May 136SuperCatInvite remaining admin team usersDONE7SuperCatShare core data file referencesATTACHED8SuperCatSend email template breakdown (what to customize for ML vs NSL branding)SEE BELOW9SuperCatConfigure PO number and ship-to as required fields; set default order type to QuoteBefore May 1310SuperCatConfigure default price levels — ML Reps default to ML Net (with DealerNet accessible but not default); NSL Reps default to NSL Net (same setup)This weekA couple of things to start thinking about before the 13th:Presentations &amp; Smart Lists — We touched on pre-built presentation formats (3x3, 9x9) and smart lists (new introductions, discontinued items, top sellers by territory, stocking suggestions, etc.). If you have ideas for how you'd want those organized for your reps, bring them to the next session and we can get them configured.Order Email Recipients — When you're updating the Order Email Recipient template for each user group (details below), remember that Jen P mentioned wanting ML and NSL orders routed to two separate people internally. Make sure each brand's user group has the correct CC address set so nothing lands in the wrong inbox.EMAIL TEMPLATES — Action NeededYour eCat account currently has default/placeholder email templates that need to be customized before ordering and enrollment go live. Since you have two brands (Magic Lite and NSL) with separate user groups, the order-related templates need a version for each brand.All of the %placeholder% fields (like %orderNumber%, %buyerFirstName%, etc.) are automatically filled in by the system — leave those as-is. You only need to update the spots with bracketed text or placeholder values like ### ### ####.ORDER EMAIL TEMPLATES These are set per user group. You'll need to update them in two places:Admin Console → Users → User Groups → ML Reps → Email TemplatesAdmin Console → Users → User Groups → NSL Reps → Email TemplatesIn each user group, update the following templates:Order Email Message and Order Email Message (prices hidden):Replace ### ### #### with that brand's customer service phone numberReplace the terms &amp; conditions URL with that brand's actual T&amp;C link, or remove it if not neededReview the disclaimer paragraph at the bottom and adjust to match that brand's order policiesOrder Email Recipient:Set the email address where order copies should be sent for each brandOrder Email Subject:Customize per brand if desired, or leave as-isOrder Item Line and Order Item Line (prices hidden):These can typically be left as-is unless you want to change what appears per line itemENROLLMENT TEMPLATES These are set at the organization level (one version shared across both brands):Admin Console → Organization → Email TemplatesEnrollment Approval Email:Replace [company name] with your preferred company name (appears twice)Replace [insert eOL login URL here] with your eCat Online login URLReplace [eCat admin name], [eCat admin email address], and [eCat admin phone number] with the contact info for whoever should be in the sign-offEnrollment Rejection Email:Replace [company name] with your preferred company name (appears three times)Replace [insert eOL public site URL here] with your public eCat Online catalog URLReplace [email address] and [phone] with your contact infoReminder: For any one-off questions going forward, the best way to reach us is support@supercatsolutions.com.Please let me know if I've missed anything or if any of the above needs adjusting.Best regards, Kyla &amp; Kylor
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14456 [tier A] — 2026-05-05T16:40:18+00:00 — Re: SuperCat invite - Please activate account within 7 days

*From:* kyla@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* magiclite.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* l1 - handled by frontline support, product - admin console, s4 - low, type: user management

```
Hi Jen,Thanks for reaching out. Jason and Michelle both completed their enrollment today, so they're all set. Two people still need to activate:&nbsp;Tom Penton&nbsp;&nbsp;and&nbsp;Pansy Ali&nbsp;. If their invitations have expired, you can resend them from the Admin Console under Users &gt; Invitations.On the username issues, I completely understand the frustration. The invitation system is a newer feature and we're actively collecting feedback to improve it. Here's what's happening and what your team can do in the meantime:Why "jfisher" was taken:Usernames are shared across all SuperCat clients globally, not just Magic Lite. So common combinations like "jfisher" or "jsmith" may already belong to someone at a completely different company. Unfortunately, the system doesn't currently check for this when the invitation is created; the user only finds out during enrollment.Username Guidelines (feel free to forward to your team):Use&nbsp;letters, numbers, and hyphens only. Do not use periods (.) as they cause errors.Your username&nbsp;cannot be changed after enrollment, so choose carefully.If your first choice is taken, try: firstname-lastname (e.g., jason-fisher), firstnamelastname (e.g., jasonfisher), or firstnameinitial (e.g., jasonf).Your username is only for login. Your display name in the app is always your full name.If someone gets stuck:You can delete the invitation from the Admin Console and resend a new one with updated details. If you run into any trouble with that, just let me know and I'll take care of it.I've logged this feedback with our engineering team so we can make the experience smoother for future invitations.Best regards, Kyla
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14418 [tier A] — 2026-05-19T21:04:55+00:00 — Re: SuperCat / Magic Lite: 4/29 Follow-Up

*From:* kyla@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* magiclite.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* l1 - handled by frontline support, product - admin console, s4 - low, type: training

```
Hi Brittni and Jen,Following up from our call today. Based on how you want Magic Lite and NSL reps to see only their respective inventory, we have configured Distribution Centers in SuperCat to handle this separation. We have already set this up and tested it on our end, and we are attaching the corrected inventory file so you can see the exact format we need going forward.Here is the recording for your reference:https://fathom.video/share/aMcEAKXGctpt9YCK8xyuP3fCn5JmMxuvHow It WorksWe have set up two Distribution Centers in SuperCat: one for Magic Lite (ml ) and one for NSL (nsl ). Each user group is assigned to its respective distribution center, so Magic Lite reps only see Magic Lite inventory and NSL reps only see NSL inventory.How Distribution Centers WorkIn SuperCat, a Distribution Center represents a distinct source of inventory. When the system imports inventory data, each row is tagged to a specific Distribution Center based on the DistributionCenter &nbsp;column value in the file. On the backend, when a rep syncs their iPad, the server looks up which Distribution Center(s) their user group is authorized to see, then sums only the inventory rows that belong to those centers. So an ML rep signed into the Magic Lite user group will only receive inventory rows tagged to the ML &nbsp;distribution center, and an NSL rep will only receive rows tagged to NSL .This is why the file format needed to change. The previous format had one row per SKU with brand-specific columns (e.g., ML_qtyavailable , NSL_qtyavailable ), but SuperCat's filtering engine works at the row level, not the column level. By splitting each SKU into two rows (one per distribution center), the system can filter, sum, and serve the correct quantities to each user group automatically.What Changes in the Inventory FileLooking at the way you want to present your data to reps, we recommend structuring the inventory file with two rows per product and a DistributionCenter &nbsp;column (instead of one row with separate ML/NSL columns). This is the format that SuperCat uses to filter inventory by user group. The file should still be named inventory.csv &nbsp;and uploaded via SFTP as before.We have attached the corrected file to this email so you can use it as a reference for the GP export script. Here is the format:BaseItemCode,DistributionCenter,QtyAvailable,QtyOnHand,QtyReserved,QtyOnBackorder,QtyOnPOrder SL-ID-30K-LP-20-A8,NSL,375,379,4,0,0 SL-ID-30K-LP-20-A8,ML,-1,3,4,8,150 The column mapping from the current file is:
  
Current ColumnNew Column NameNSL_qtyavailable / ML_qtyavailableQtyAvailableNSL_qtyonhand / ML_qtyonhandQtyOnHandNSL_qtyreserved / ML_qtyreservedQtyReservedNSL_qtybackordered / ML_qtybackorderedQtyOnBackorderNSL_qtyonporder / ML_qtyonporderQtyOnPOrderWe have already converted your current inventory data to this format and imported it successfully. The attached file contains all 694 SKUs with both ML and NSL rows. Please use this as the template for the GP export script going forward.Action Items
  
#OwnerAction ItemETA1BrittniUpdate the GP export script to output inventory in the new format (two rows per product with DistributionCenter column set to NSL &nbsp;or ML )May 232Brittni / JenReview the imported inventory quantities in SuperCat to confirm the data looks correctMay 213BrittniOnce the script is updated, send a test file via SFTP so we can verify the automated flow end to endMay 234BrittniSales history file: load once for initial data, then reload before beta launch and before full launch (not a daily feed)Before beta launchSales History FileAs discussed on the call, the sales history file is not a daily feed. We need it loaded once for the initial data, then reloaded before beta launch and again before full launch. The file should be named sales_data.csv &nbsp;and uploaded via SFTP. Here are the required columns:
  
ColumnRequiredTypeDescriptionBaseItemCodeYesTextProduct SKU (must match an active product in SuperCat)BillToCodeYesTextCustomer account code (must match a customer in SuperCat)QuantityInvoiced*IntegerNumber of units invoicedAmountInvoicedNoDecimalDollar amount invoicedShipToCodeNoTextShip-to location code (if applicable)DisplayQuantityNoIntegerQuantity to display on the iPad (if different from invoiced)EstShipDateNoDateEstimated ship date for open ordersQtyOnOrder*IntegerQuantity currently on orderAmountOnOrderNoDecimalDollar amount on order*Either QuantityInvoiced or QtyOnOrder must be provided per row (at least one is required).This data powers the "Customer Favorites" feature on the iPad, which shows reps what each customer has purchased before. It also feeds the Sales Portal dashboards. For full details, tips, and examples, see our KB articles:Publishing Sales Data (quick reference)Publishing Summary Sales Data (full guide with SmartPicks, sorting by sales rank, and auto-generation options)Customer Sales Summaries (how reps use Favorites, On Order, On Display, and SmartPicks on the iPad)One tip from the KB: the first line of the file can contain a :PARAMS &nbsp;line to display a title in eCat, e.g.::PARAMS,"description=Sales Data from 01/01/24 to 12/31/25 (last updated 05/19/26)" Inventory Sync FrequencyWe recommend a daily sync (overnight or early morning) so reps start each day with fresh inventory numbers. Can the GP export be scheduled to run automatically to the SFTP, or will this be a manual upload initially? If manual to start, a few times per week works until you automate it.Product FiltersWe have enabled the following product filters on the iPad: Color Temperature, Wattage, Application, and Mounting Type. Are these the right fields for your reps to filter by, or would you like to add or remove any?Please let me know if you have any questions about the new format or if anything needs adjusting.Best regards,Kyla
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14727 [tier A] — 2026-07-06T15:03:01+00:00 — RE: SuperCat / Magic Lite: 4/29 Follow-Up

*From:* kyla@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* magiclite.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* status: support project

```
Hi Jen, Jen P and Brittany,I have sent over the meeting invites for tomorrow.@Brittany, I had a look into your query. Two things need to change in the PowerShell script:1. Filename must be&nbsp;inventory.csv The SuperCat SFTP server only accepts specific filenames for each data type. For inventory data, the file must be uploaded as exactly:inventory.csv
Not&nbsp;ItemQuantities_20260706_094423.csv . The server handles versioning and timestamping on its side after it processes the file, so you don't need to include a date in the filename.2. Upload pathThe remote path in the script should be:/inventory.csv
or just:./inventory.csv
No subdirectories, just the filename in the root of the SFTP home directory.In your SFTP.ps1 script, look for the line that sets the remote file path (likely something like&nbsp;$remotePath &nbsp;or the destination in your&nbsp;PutFiles &nbsp;or&nbsp;TransferFiles &nbsp;call) and change it from the dynamic&nbsp;ItemQuantities_YYYYMMDD_*.csv &nbsp;pattern to a static&nbsp;inventory.csv .For reference, here are the expected filenames for each data type on the mali SFTP:
  
Data TypeExpected FilenameInventoryinventory.csv Productsproducts.csv Customerscustomers.csv Sales Datasales_data.csv Once you update the filename and re-run the script, it should upload without the permission error. Let me know if you hit any other issues.Best regards, Kyla
```

```
Hey Kyla,
    
    Thanks for your time today during the meeting. Please see the attached file that we are trying to move as well as a snippet of the powershell script moving to the SFTP site. Some of the connection information has been removed from the powershell script we are using.
    
    ***************************************************************************************************************************************************************************
    
    # SQL Connection Information
    
    $ServerName =
    
    $DatabaseName =
    
    # Local Export Folder
    
    $ExportFolder = "C:\Exports"
    
    $Timestamp = Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"
    
    $CsvFile = "$ExportFolder\inventory.csv"
    
    # SFTP Settings
    
    $SftpHost = "sftp2.supercatsolutions.com"
    
    $SftpUser = "mali"
    
    $SftpPassword = "9vjzsAw268Fu"
    
    $SftpRemotePath = "inventory.csv"
    
    # WinSCP .NET Assembly Path
    
    $WinSCPDll = "C:\Program Files (x86)\WinSCP\WinSCPnet.dll"
    
    # SQL Query
    
    $Query = @"
    
    "@
    
    # Ensure export folder exists
    
    if (!(Test-Path $ExportFolder)) {
    
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExportFolder | Out-Null
    
    }
    
    # Run SQL Query and Export CSV
    
    $ConnectionString = "Server=$ServerName;Database=$DatabaseName;Integrated Security=True;"
    
    $Connection = New-Object System.Data.SqlClient.SqlConnection $ConnectionString
    
    $Command = New-Object System.Data.SqlClient.SqlCommand $Query, $Connection
    
    $Adapter = New-Object System.Data.SqlClient.SqlDataAdapter $Command
    
    $DataTable = New-Object System.Data.DataTable
    
    try {
    
    $Connection.Open()
    
    $Adapter.Fill($DataTable) | Out-Null
    
    }
    
    finally {
    
    if ($Connection.State -eq 'Open') {
    
    $Connection.Close()
    
    }
    
    }
    
    $DataTable | Export-Csv -Path $CsvFile -NoTypeInformation
    
    # Upload to SFTP
    
    Add-Type -Path $WinSCPDll
    
    $SessionOptions = New-Object WinSCP.SessionOptions -Property @{
    
    Protocol = [WinSCP.Protocol]::Sftp
    
    HostName = $SftpHost
    
    UserName = $SftpUser
    
    Password = $SftpPassword
    
    GiveUpSecurityAndAcceptAnySshHostKey = $true
    
    }
    
    $Session = New-Object WinSCP.Session
    
    try {
    
    $Session.Open($SessionOptions)
    
    $TransferOptions = New-Object WinSCP.TransferOptions
    
    $TransferOptions.TransferMode = [WinSCP.TransferMode]::Binary
    
    $TransferResult = $Session.PutFiles($CsvFile, $SftpRemotePath, $false, $TransferOptions)
    
    $TransferResult.Check()
    
    Write-Host "Export complete: $CsvFile"
    
    Write-Host "SFTP upload complete: $SftpRemotePath"
    
    }
    
    finally {
    
    $Session.Dispose()
    
    }

Brittni Pryputniski

+ E-mail: bpryputniski@endeavoursolutions.com

8 Web: https://www.endeavoursolutions.com/ | https://www.gpsupportnorth.com/
```


## ✉️ TICKET #14727 [tier A] — 2026-07-07T19:32:53+00:00 — RE: SuperCat / Magic Lite: 4/29 Follow-Up

*From:* kyla@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* magiclite.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* status: support project

```
Hi Brittni,Thanks for sending through the script. I can see the issue.The SFTP remote path needs to point to the&nbsp;/data/ &nbsp;directory, not the root. Your script is currently uploading to the root directory (/inventory.csv ), where the "mali user" doesn't have write access. Files need to go to&nbsp;/data/inventory.csv .Change this line:$SftpRemotePath = "inventory.csv"
to:$SftpRemotePath = "/data/inventory.csv"
Also, explicitly setting port 22 is good practice to make sure you're connecting via SFTP (not FTP on port 21). You can add&nbsp;PortNumber = 22 &nbsp;to your SessionOptions block:$SessionOptions = New-Object WinSCP.SessionOptions -Property @{
    Protocol = [WinSCP.Protocol]::Sftp
    HostName = $SftpHost
    PortNumber = 22
    UserName = $SftpUser
    Password = $SftpPassword
    GiveUpSecurityAndAcceptAnySshHostKey = $true
}
Those two changes should resolve the "Permission denied" error. Once you've updated the script, try running it again and let me know if it goes through.One other thing to keep in mind for the inventory file: all quantity values need to be whole numbers (integers). If your GP export produces decimal values like 258.5, round them before export. The SuperCat importer will reject rows with non-integer quantities.Best regards, Kyla
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14727 [tier A] — 2026-07-08T22:39:51+00:00 — RE: SuperCat / Magic Lite: 4/29 Follow-Up

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* magiclite.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* status: support project

```
@kylaB Email to Magic Lite for my outstanding items: Updates from our sideProduct Stories&nbsp;We've re-imported the stories file and all 695 products now have a product story populated. We're also re-scraping the website to improve on the initial versions pulled from the PDF catalog — that updated file will replace the current one once complete.Spec Sheets / Cut Sheets&nbsp;Apologies for any confusion here — we were initially adding these to the media library, but the better path is custom fields that reference them directly on the product detail page. We're in the process of scraping and mapping those now and expect to have it done by end of week.Items We Need Your Help With1. Pricing — Action RequiredWe dug into this more thoroughly to make sure we had the full picture before reaching out. Here's what's actually happening:The setup is correct.&nbsp;Your ML and NSL user groups are intentionally configured so reps only see their respective dealer net pricing — they won't see list price or net price. That's exactly what you asked for, and it's working as designed.The data gap is in the source files.&nbsp;The two price lists you sent us (the ML and NSL August 2025 files) are your&nbsp;discontinued LTP-series&nbsp;line — "While Quantities Last" products. Those lists gave us pricing for roughly&nbsp;81–87 items&nbsp;in the catalog, and we've imported those correctly.The remaining&nbsp;607+ products&nbsp;— your current active lineup, including the LV, LVLDL, LEDMD, LTSPRO, LES, ES, NFLX series and others — were never in those files. They aren't missing because of a build error on our end; they simply weren't included in the price lists we received. As a result, reps in both user groups see blank prices on the vast majority of the catalog, because the ML and NSL price levels have nothing populated to display.What we need:&nbsp;A current, complete price list from Magic Lite covering all active SKUs — for both divisions:ML price list&nbsp;(Canadian division) — List price and Dealer Net per item codeNSL price list&nbsp;(US division) — List price and Dealer Net per item codeIf your internal item codes differ from the eCat codes, include both so we can map them. Once we have these, we can build out the pricing columns across all 694 products and re-import.In the meantime — a temporary option:&nbsp;There is a quick workaround available while you track down the full price lists. Your catalog already has a&nbsp;net_price &nbsp;column populated for 652 of 694 products, and that value equals your ML list price. We could temporarily authorize that price level in the ML and NSL user groups so reps can at least see&nbsp;something&nbsp;on the majority of products while we wait. The trade-off is that reps would see the ML list price labeled as their net — not ideal, but it keeps the catalog usable. Let us know if you'd like us to enable that as a stopgap.2. eCat OnlineFor your eCat Online setup, we need one decision:&nbsp;do you want a custom domain?This determines the URL your reps and customers will use to access the online catalog (e.g.&nbsp;catalog.magiclite.com &nbsp;vs. a default SuperCat-hosted URL). A custom domain looks more professional and matches your brand, but it requires a DNS record update on your end. Here's how each path works:Custom domain&nbsp;(e.g.&nbsp;catalog.magiclite.com ): Let us know the hostname you'd like and who on your team handles DNS. We'll send the exact CNAME entries to add once setup is underway — it typically takes just a few minutes on your end.SuperCat-hosted URL: If you'd prefer to test and launch on a default URL first, we can revisit the custom domain later. This is a fine option if getting the DNS sorted quickly isn't practical right now.No urgency yet — just want to know which direction you're heading so we can sequence the setup correctly.3. Email Templates — Action RequiredYour eCat account currently has default placeholder email templates that need to be customized before ordering and rep enrollment go live. Since you have two brands (Magic Lite and NSL) with separate user groups, you'll need to do this in two places:Admin Console → Users → User Groups →&nbsp;ML Reps&nbsp;→ Email TemplatesAdmin Console → Users → User Groups →&nbsp;NSL Reps&nbsp;→ Email TemplatesIn each user group, update the following:
  
TemplateWhat to UpdateOrder Email Message&nbsp;and&nbsp;Order Email Message (prices hidden)Replace&nbsp;### ### #### &nbsp;with that brand's customer service phone number; update or remove the T&amp;C link; review the disclaimer paragraph for that brand's order policiesOrder Email RecipientThe email address where order copies should be sent for that brandOrder Email SubjectCustomize per brand if desired, or leave as-isOrder Item Line&nbsp;and&nbsp;Order Item Line (prices hidden)Typically fine to leave as-is unless you want to change per-line-item displayAll&nbsp;%placeholder% &nbsp;fields (like&nbsp;%orderNumber% ,&nbsp;%buyerFirstName% , etc.) are filled in automatically by the system — leave those alone. You're only updating spots with bracketed text or&nbsp;### ### #### &nbsp;placeholders.The pricing item is the biggest open gate right now — once we have the current price lists, we can close that out quickly. Happy to jump on a call if any of this is easier to walk through live.
```

Here's what's actually happening:The setup is correct. Your ML and NSL user groups are intentionally configured so reps only see their respective dealer net pricing; they won't see list price or net price. That's exactly what you asked for, and it's working as designed.The data gap is in the source files. The two price lists you sent us (the ML and NSL August 2025 files) are your discontinued LTP-series line, "While Quantities Last" products. Those lists gave us pricing for roughly 81 to 87 items in the catalog, and we've imported those correctly.The remaining 607+ products (your current active lineup, including the LV, LVLDL, LEDMD, LTSPRO, LES, ES, NFLX series and others) were never in those files. Once we have these, we can build out the pricing columns across all 694 products and re-import.In the meantime, we can suggest a temporary option: Your catalog already has a net_price column populated for 652 of 694 products, and that value equals your ML list price. We could temporarily authorize that price level in the ML and NSL user groups so reps can at least see something on the majority of products while we wait. The trade-off is that reps would see the ML list price labeled as their net, which is not ideal, but it keeps the catalog usable. Let us know if you'd like us to enable that as a "stop-gap".eCat Online DomainFor your eCat Online setup, would you like a custom domain?This determines the URL your reps and customers will use to access the online catalog (e.g. catalog.magiclite.com vs. a default SuperCat-hosted URL). A custom domain looks more professional and matches your brand, but it requires a DNS record update on your end. Here's how each path works:Custom domain (e.g. catalog.magiclite.com): Let us know the hostname you'd like and who on your team handles DNS. We'll send the exact CNAME entries to add once setup is underway; it typically takes just a few minutes on your end.SuperCat-hosted URL: If you'd prefer to test and launch on a default URL first, we can revisit the custom domain later. This is a fine option if getting the DNS sorted quickly isn't practical right now.No urgency yet; just want to know which direction you're heading so we can sequence the setup correctly.Email Templates (Action Required)Your eCat account currently has default placeholder email templates that need to be customized before ordering and rep enrollment go live. Since you have two brands (Magic Lite and NSL) with separate user groups, you'll need to do this in two places:Admin Console &gt; Users &gt; User Groups &gt; ML Reps &gt; Email TemplatesAdmin Console &gt; Users &gt; User Groups &gt; NSL Reps &gt; Email TemplatesIn each user group, update the following:Order Email Message and Order Email Message (prices hidden): Replace ### ### #### with that brand's customer service phone number; update or remove the T&amp;C link; review the disclaimer paragraph for that brand's order policies.Order Email Recipient: The email address where order copies should be sent for that brand.Order Email Subject: Customize per brand if desired, or leave as-is.Order Item Line and Order Item Line (prices hidden): Typically fine to leave as-is unless you want to change per-line-item display.All %placeholder% fields (like %orderNumber%, %buyerFirstName%, etc.) are filled in automatically by the system; leave those alone. You're only updating spots with bracketed text or ### ### #### placeholders.What We'd Like You To Do NextWhile we finish up the pricing and remaining items on our side, we'd love for you to start reviewing what's already in place:Log into the Admin Console and review the setup: user groups, permissions, product data, and any settings that look off or need adjusting.Log into the iPad app, sync, and check that the catalog, product details, images, and navigation all look correct.Try out eCat Online and confirm the branding, layout, and product visibility look right.Make notes of anything you'd like to change, customize, or add (layout preferences, additional fields, filtering options, anything at all) so we can discuss it in our next meeting once pricing is complete and the account is fully built out.This doesn't need to be exhaustive; just a first pass so we can hit the ground running in our next session.Brittni: Import Follow-UpBrittni, can you give us a quick update on the inventory and sales data imports? Specifically:Were you able to resolve the SFTP upload issue we discussed (the remote path needed to be /data/inventory.csv)?Any progress on the sales data export from GP?If you're still running into issues, just let us know and we can hop on a quick call to sort it out.Action Items
  
```
#ActionOwnerPriority1Send current, complete ML price list (List + Dealer Net for all active SKUs)Magic LiteHigh2Send current, complete NSL price list (List + Dealer Net for all active SKUs)Magic LiteHigh3Let us know: enable temporary net_price display as a stopgap? (Yes/No)Magic LiteMedium4Let us know: custom domain for eCat Online, or use default URL for now?Magic LiteMedium5Update Email Templates in Admin Console for ML Reps (phone number, T&amp;C, order email recipient)Magic LiteMedium6Update Email Templates in Admin Console for NSL Reps (phone number, T&amp;C, order email recipient)Magic LiteMedium7Log into Admin Console, iPad, and eCat Online; make notes on anything to change or customizeMagic LiteMedium8Update on inventory SFTP upload and sales data export from GPBrittniMedium9Finish scraping and mapping spec sheets/cut sheetsSuperCatIn progress eta Friday10Import full pricing once price lists are receivedSuperCatWaiting on #1 and #2In Summary:The pricing item is the biggest open gate right now. Specifically:	* Were you able to resolve the SFTP upload issue we discussed (theremote path needed to be /data/inventory.csv)?	* Any progress on the sales data export from GP?If you're still running into issues, just let us know and we can hopon a quick call to sort it out.ACTION ITEMS#ActionOwnerPriority1Send current, complete ML price list (List + Dealer Net for all activeSKUs)Magic LiteHigh2Send current, complete NSL price list (List + Dealer Net for allactive SKUs)Magic LiteHigh3Let us know: enable temporary net_price display as a stopgap? This is exactly the kind of feedback we need before launch.I've looked into both items and I believe the cause is the same for both: your account is set up as an Admin user, which gives you visibility into content for both the Magic Lite and NSL brands simultaneously. A regular NSL rep would only see NSL pricing (in USD) and NSL-branded cut sheets/instructions.One option to make your review easier: you can temporarily move your user profile to the NSL Reps group (or ML Reps) so you can see exactly what your reps will see on each site. I can switch your profile over and we can confirm everything is displaying correctly from a rep's perspective.In the meantime, we are updating the prices from the list you sent through, and I have added the library option to the NSL catalog. &nbsp;If you or the team have any other feedback from your review, please send it along, and we'll add it to the agenda for Wednesday.Best regards, Kyla
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14727 [tier A] — 2026-07-24T17:01:38+00:00 — RE: SuperCat / Magic Lite: 4/29 Follow-Up

*From:* kyla@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* magiclite.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* status: support project

```
Hi Jen Z, Jen P and Brittany,Thank you for the thorough review and all the time your team has put into testing. That level of detail is exactly what helps us get this right, and it's clear your team knows these products inside and out.The feedback loop from this week has been really productive. We've been able to close out a significant number of items, and the system is in a much stronger place because of it. Several of the concerns raised were actually confirmation that things are built correctly; they just weren't visible through the right lens. More on that below.Here's a full breakdown of where things stand.Highlights / TakeawaysA major milestone from this week was aligning on how user groups and permissions work. With two distinct catalogs running under one instance, user group permissions are the mechanism that controls what each person sees. The Admin login shows everything from both brands combined, which is by design, but it was understandably causing confusion during review. Once viewed through the correct rep profile, the brand separation is clean and working as intended:ML and NSL each have their own pricing, cut sheets, instructions, Library documents, and UPC codes, all correctly assigned to their respective user groups.Customer DN (cost) pricing now displays by default when a rep logs in. List pricing is available as a toggle. No manual switching needed.NSL pricing is configured in USD. Canadian pricing includes a CAD  prefix (e.g., CAD $53.30  ); if you don't see the prefix, you're viewing USD pricing $  .To make it easy to verify all of this firsthand, we've set up dedicated test profiles for you both:Your User Test Profiles:
  
PersonEmailPasswordJen Zjen+user@magiclite.compassword2026!Jen Pjen.penton+user@magiclite.compassword2026!These accounts are currently assigned to the NSL Reps user group. When you log in (on iPad or eCat Online), you'll see NSL pricing, NSL cut sheets, and NSL Library documents only.To view the Magic Lite side: -&gt; Go to Admin Console &gt; Users &gt; Users, [https://supercat.supercatsolutions.com/mali/org_users]-&gt; Find your test profile, and click on your username. Switch the user group from NSL Reps to ML Reps &gt; Save. -&gt; Then sync the iPad app or log out and back in on eCat Online. -&gt; Switch it back to NSL Reps when you want to return to the NSL view.Your Admin accounts remain unchanged for all admin tasks.Below is a list of items that have been updated/completed or are in progress.What We've Completed:
  
ItemDetailAdmin view showing mixed brandsAdmin-only behavior; Resolved by using rep test profiles (see above)Cut sheets/instructions showing wrong brandingCorrectly separated by user group; confirmed in database. NSL products display NSL-branded documents (from nslusa.com), ML products display ML-branded documents (from magiclite.com). Library mixing ML and NSL contentFully separated; ML docs visible only to ML groups, NSL only to NSLUPC codes showing both brandsAdmin-only behavior; rep profiles show only the relevant brand's UPCNSL pricing showing CADNSL price levels are configured in USDNSL pricing currencyNSL pricing is configured in USD. The system displays a $ symbol for both currencies, but Canadian pricing includes the CAD prefix (e.g., CAD $53.30 ). If you don't see the CAD prefix, you're viewing USD pricing.Customer pricing not defaulting to DNDefault price levels corrected for both ML Reps and NSL Reps and eCat Online. List pricing is available as a toggle in the price selector. Imported the final product file Applied 130 image fixes, added UPC codes, and updated pricing.Brand-specific inventory fieldsConfigured and populated (ML and NSL quantities tracked separately)Inventory custom fields not displaying on eCat OnlineFixed and displayingWhat's Still in Progress:
  
ItemOwnerStatusETARemaining image items are detailed in the attached Excel (Shared Images tab)Mali teamExcel attached with full list of SKUs, what's wrong, and what we need. Please review and provide corrected image files.Awaiting client input (Excel attached)Full pricing gap list is in the attached Excel (108 items). 18 of these were already addressed from Jen's July 15 response and are marked accordingly.Mali teamExcel attached (Pricing Gaps tab). Please fill in the highlighted yellow cells and return.Awaiting client input (Excel attached, yellow cells)99 discontinued products to hide/removeMali teamIn progress. List in the Excel for your reference.Awaiting client input (Excel attached)~200 legacy products with no pricingMali teamExcel attached (Legacy Products tab). Please review and confirm which should be kept, hidden, or removed.Awaiting client input (Excel attached)Pack size/unit of measure (UOM) for tape productsSuperCat + Mali teamIn progress. We may need to clarify some details in the inventory file with Brittni.Tape descriptions are updated (spool length now in product name). UOM custom field needs clarification in our meeting.NSL item codes (57 differ from ML)SuperCat teamMapping complete. Building NSL code field visible to NSL reps only.At next Meeting SyncSFTP for BrittniSuperCat + Mali teamSentI've forwarded the SFTP connection details. There's currently a permission-denied error on Brittni's IT side that needs resolving before automated uploads can start.The Excel file with all details "Mali_Product_Review_Workbook.xlsx"  (mismatched images, pricing gaps, discontinued products, and legacy products) is attached. Please review ahead of our next meeting so we can work through any questions together.Brittni: Inventory File UpdateBrittni, we've restructured the inventory file to support brand-specific quantities (ML and NSL tracked separately). I'll forward the SFTP connection details to you separately so you have everything in one place. Could you review the updated file format (inventory.csv: attached) and let me know if it works on your end for the automated upload?It would also be really helpful to have you join our next meeting so we can walk through the file setup and SFTP process together. If the group meeting time doesn't work, I'm happy to schedule a separate call with you. Just let me know what works best for your schedule and I'll make it happen.Suggested: Weekly Check-InTo keep momentum and make sure nothing gets stuck, I'd like to suggest a tentative weekly standing meeting until rep-launch, which we can discuss during our next meeting.Availability next week EST:I know Jen P is currently away. When would be the best time for us to meet at your earliest convenience to discuss the next steps forward and align on mutually agreed milestones? Tuesday 28th: 11:00 - 15:30Wednesday 29th: 11:00 - 13:30 | 15:00 - 16:00We're looking forward to reviewing this together at our next sync and getting everything across the finish line, so your team can start using the system with confidence.Warm regards, Kylor &amp; Kyla.
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14842 [tier A] — 2026-07-27T15:31:30+00:00 — SFTP Permissions

*From:* kyla@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* endeavoursolutions.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* status: support project

```
Hi Brittni,Please see the forwarded email below. Kindly confirm if this resolved the issue. Thanks for sending through the script. I can see the issue.The SFTP remote path needs to point to the&nbsp;/data/ &nbsp;directory, not the root. Your script is currently uploading to the root directory (/inventory.csv &nbsp;), where the "mali user" doesn't have write access. Files need to go to&nbsp;/data/inventory.csv &nbsp;.Change this line:$SftpRemotePath = "inventory.csv"
to:$SftpRemotePath = "/data/inventory.csv"
Also, explicitly setting port 22 is good practice to make sure you're connecting via SFTP (not FTP on port 21). You can add&nbsp;PortNumber = 22 &nbsp;to your SessionOptions block:$SessionOptions = New-Object WinSCP.SessionOptions -Property @{
    Protocol = [WinSCP.Protocol]::Sftp
    HostName = $SftpHost
    PortNumber = 22
    UserName = $SftpUser
    Password = $SftpPassword
    GiveUpSecurityAndAcceptAnySshHostKey = $true
}
Those two changes should resolve the "Permission denied" error. Once you've updated the script, try running it again and let me know if it goes through.One other thing to keep in mind for the inventory file: all quantity values need to be whole numbers (integers). If your GP export produces decimal values like 258.5, round them before export. The SuperCat importer will reject rows with non-integer quantities.Best regards,Kyla
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14891 [tier A] — 2026-08-06T17:17:41+00:00 — RE: SFTP Permissions

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* endeavoursolutions.com · *status:* active · *tags:* l1 - handled by frontline support

```
Hi Brittni, Quick one on that inventory import — the file failed because the column header in your export doesn't match what eCat expects.Your file has:Item Number eCat requires:BaseItemCode The importer looks for that exact name (it's case-insensitive, so&nbsp;baseitemcode &nbsp;is fine, but it has to be that word). Since it couldn't find it, it rejected the whole file.Fix:&nbsp;In your export process, rename that first column from&nbsp;Item Number &nbsp;to&nbsp;BaseItemCode , then re-import A few other column names in your file will also need renaming to match eCat's expected headers (QTY On Hand &nbsp;→&nbsp;QtyOnHand ,&nbsp;QTY Available &nbsp;→&nbsp;QtyAvailable , etc.). I can send you the full mapping if helpful, or if you want to send me the file I can rename the headers for you.Let me know!
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14891 [tier A] — 2026-08-10T16:57:37+00:00 — RE: SFTP Permissions

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* endeavoursolutions.com · *status:* active · *tags:* l1 - handled by frontline support

```
Hi Brittni, We received your inventory file this morning (inventory.csv, 703 rows). The upload ran, but eCat rejected every quantity row because of format issues, so the old data is still displaying. Here's what to fix:Quantity values must be whole numbers.Your export has values like 97.00000 and 0.00000. eCat requires integers with no decimals, e.g. 97 and 0. This affected all 703 data rows and is the reason the import failed.Four SKUs also have fractional on-hand in the export (74.5, 178.5, 258.5, 340.5). Please round those in your source system, or tell us your rounding rule and we can apply it. One SKU (NFLX-RGB-CONKIT-6FT) shows -1 on hand, which will need to be corrected to 0 or the actual count.Column headers need to match eCat exactly.Several headers in the file are ERP labels eCat doesn't recognize (these showed as warnings). Your export column first, what eCat expects below:Item Descriptionnot used in inventory, remove itItem Class CodeItemClassCodeLocation CodeLocationCodeQTY On OrderML_QtyOnPOrderQtyOnHandML_QtyOnHandQTY AllocatedML_QtyReservedQtyAvailableML_QtyAvailableQTY Back OrderedML_QtyOnBackorderInclude both ML and NSL quantity columns on one row per SKU.Your file is ML-warehouse only (all rows show Location Code ML). For eCat we need one row per BaseItemCode with both sets of columns: the ML_* fields from your Magic Lite export and the matching NSL_* fields from NSL. Use 0 where a warehouse has no stock.The columns, in order:BaseItemCodeQtyAvailableNSL_QtyOnPOrderNSL_QtyOnHandNSL_QtyReservedNSL_QtyAvailableNSL_QtyOnBackorderML_QtyOnPOrderML_QtyOnHandML_QtyReservedML_QtyAvailableML_QtyOnBackorderReference: Inventory FileRe-upload when ready.I've attached the reformatted version for your reference as well. The current columns you imported are on the first row and the SuperCat formatted version we need is below. Best,Kylor
```
